# Regional Center Directives : CA Department of Developmental Services

**Source:** [https://www.dds.ca.gov/rc/regional-center-directives/](https://www.dds.ca.gov/rc/regional-center-directives/)

---

DDS frequently distributes guidance and instructions on policy issues to regional centers (RCs). Documents marked as **Directives** are instructions RCs must follow. **Program Guidelines** are rules RCs must follow for specific programs. **Guidance** documents share details on items RCs are encouraged to do or approaches to consider, but are not mandated. **Correspondence** documents are how DDS shares helpful information with the RCs. All RC directives, correspondence, guidance, and program guidelines are posted on this page.
